在积极方面，我们表明，如果预言机对其每个答案附加密码学签名，那么只需假设抗碰撞哈希函数，每个预言机辅助计算都可以用高效的证明者和验证者进行零知识验证。除隐私之外，这也提供了一种可扩展监督的替代方法，它既不依赖于辩论中的诚实对手，也不依赖于先前单证明者协议中的计算鲁棒性。

Original abstract: AI systems increasingly produce outputs from confidential data, such as a fitness-for-duty assessment from medical records or the predicted properties of a drug candidate from its secret structure. It is important to verify that such outputs are correct without revealing the underlying data. A recent line of work studies verification of AI outputs via interactive proofs and debate for oracle-aided computation, where correctness may depend on an oracle such as human judgment, a physical experiment, or the web. These works focus on verification by a verifier that runs much faster than the computation. However, such efficient verification is impossible for general oracle-aided computation, and these works therefore rely on additional assumptions. We focus instead on privacy: allowing the verifier to run in time polynomial in the computation, we ask whether interactive arguments for oracle-aided computation can be zero knowledge, so that the verifier learns nothing about the confidential data beyond the correctness of the output.
  We prove that, in general, they cannot. In the random oracle model, there are no zero-knowledge proofs for all oracle-aided computations, even if both the prover and the verifier are allowed to run much longer than the computation itself. The impossibility extends to debate, a canonical model for scalable oversight.
  On the positive side, we show that if the oracle attaches a cryptographic signature to each of its answers, then every oracle-aided computation can be verified in zero knowledge with an efficient prover and verifier, assuming only collision-resistant hash functions. Beyond privacy, this also gives an alternative approach to scalable oversight that relies neither on an honest opponent, as in debate, nor on the robustness of the computation, as in prior single-prover protocols.

</details>


### [82] [Mingbird: A Local-First Agent Harness Enabling Small Open Models to Complete Real Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02001)
*Hao Wang,Ting Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 44 pages, 9 figures. Code, benchmark protocol, scoring code, and all 288 per-cell results: https://github.com/Mingbird/Mingbird-agent

Selection reason: 提出本地优先的agent harness及预填充预算、完成门控、循环检测等机制，直接对应小模型agent的工具使用与长程执行可靠性。

TL;DR: 在受控单机与第三方基准上，Mingbird 本地优先 agent harness 通过十种机制补偿小模型失败，LRAB 0.886 显著领先 goose/opencode/agent-mini，τ²-bench 0.856；但证据仅限自建基准、单机、单次评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重模型在云规模 agent harness 下难以完成真实任务；作者要证明相当部分失败源于 harness 而非模型。

Method: 提出 Mingbird（Windows/Ollama 本地优先），十种机制包括字节级净零预填充、完成门控重读、签名级循环检测；用受控单机 LRAB（4 harness × 4 模型 × 18 任务）与 τ²-bench（278 任务、3 臂）及前沿模型探测，并做留一机制消融和批次匹配比较。

Result: LRAB Mingbird 0.886 vs 0.631/0.479/0.405，288 单元全公布；τ²-bench 0.856 vs 0.791/0.737；前沿模型探测 0.997–0.478，良好脚手架差距≤0.072；消融仅方向性，同一晚重复均值变动至 0.069，完整栈 vs 文本重读配对 +0.10。

Conclusion: harness 对小模型任务完成度有实质影响；Mingbird 提供本地优先补偿方案，但受自建基准、单机和单次试验评分限制。

中文摘要：小型开放权重模型（2-9B）可在普通笔记本电脑上运行，但在云规模智能体执行框架（agent harnesses）下，它们很少能完成真实任务：工具预填充会溢出上下文，自我纠正会发散，工具演示会循环，任务会被静默放弃。我们通过一项受控的单机比较和一项第三方基准测试提供证据表明，这些失败中有相当大一部分应归因于执行框架，而非模型。我们提出 Mingbird，一个面向 Windows 和 Ollama 的本地优先智能体执行框架，其十种机制逐点补偿小模型失败形态，其中三种具有代表性：字节级净零预填充预算、在接受完成前重新读取任务的完成门控，以及签名级循环检测。在 LRAB 上，一项保持机器、模型、预算和评分固定的受控比较（4 个执行框架 × 4 个开放模型（2B-35B）× 18 个真实任务，确定性产物评分）中，Mingbird 总体达到 0.886，而 goose 为 0.631、opencode 为 0.479、agent-mini 为 0.405，全部 288 个单元均已公布；在 τ²-bench（278 个任务、三个实验臂、一个协议）上，其总分为 0.856，而其他为 0.791 和 0.737；对同样 18 个任务的前沿模型探测中，不同执行框架的得分从 0.997 到 0.478 不等，而构造良好的脚手架之间相差不超过 0.072。留一机制消融仅作为方向性结果报告：同一实验臂在同一晚的重复实验会使其均值最多移动 0.069，与每个名义单次试验 delta 的大小相当；唯一批次匹配的比较（完整机制栈对比仅文本重读）在三重复中给出可执行完成守卫的配对 +0.10。证据带有已声明的局限：自建基准、单台机器和单次试验评分。

Original abstract: Small open-weight models (2-9B) run on ordinary laptops, but under cloud-scale agent harnesses they rarely complete real tasks: tool prefill overflows the context, self-correction diverges, tool demonstrations loop, and tasks are silently abandoned. We present evidence, from a controlled single-machine comparison and one third-party benchmark, that a substantial share of these failures is attributable to the harness rather than the model. We introduce Mingbird, a local-first agent harness for Windows and Ollama whose ten mechanisms compensate point-by-point for small-model failure forms, three of them representative: a byte-level net-zero prefill budget, a finish gate that re-reads the task before accepting completion, and signature-level loop detection. On LRAB, a controlled comparison holding machine, models, budgets, and scoring fixed (4 harnesses $\times$ 4 open models (2B-35B) $\times$ 18 real tasks, deterministic artifact scoring), Mingbird reaches 0.886 overall against 0.631 (goose), 0.479 (opencode), and 0.405 (agent-mini), with all 288 cells published; on $τ^2$-bench (278 tasks, three arms, one protocol) it totals 0.856 against 0.791 and 0.737; and a frontier-model probe on the same 18 tasks spans 0.997 to 0.478 across harnesses, with well-formed scaffolds staying within 0.072 of each other. A leave-one-mechanism-out ablation is reported as directional only: same-night replications of the same arm move its mean by up to 0.069, the size of every nominal single-trial delta, and the one batch-matched comparison (full mechanism stack versus text re-read alone) gives the executable completion guards a paired +0.10 across three replications. The evidence carries stated limits: a self-built benchmark, a single machine, and single-trial scoring.

</details>


### [83] [Counting Moves, Weighing Voices: Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation for Calibrated Multi-LLM Councils under Persistent Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02005)
*Ionel Eduard Stan,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将多LLM council的辩论轨迹建模为带可靠性的论证过程，可迁移到多agent协作、投票聚合与对抗鲁棒性设计。

TL;DR: BDA将多LLM委员会的审议轨迹建模为带每智能体可靠性的标注者模型，从而给出校准置信度并提升对抗不可靠智能体的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多LLM委员会聚合方法的置信度反映果断性而非正确性，且无法识别或折扣持续不可靠的智能体。

Method: 将委员会的类型化动作（提出/质疑/让步）视为经典标注者模型观测，按推断的各智能体可靠性加权证据，输出候选答案的校准后验概率，并允许反转持续不可靠智能体。

Result: 在二分类和多分类基准上，BDA在零成本委员会聚合方法中校准最佳，无需额外LLM调用，在持续对抗联盟下鲁棒性提升，在干净设置中仍有竞争力。

Conclusion: BDA把多智能体审议转化为可靠性估计问题，可同时改善置信度校准与对持续不可靠智能体的鲁棒性。

中文摘要：多LLM委员会让若干大语言模型（LLM）就一个问题进行审议，并共同返回一个答案以及一个置信度估计。随着这些系统越来越多地用于推理，该置信度应当表示经过校准的正确概率，并且当某些智能体持续不可靠时，决策仍应保持稳健。现有的委员会聚合方法在这两方面都失败：它们的置信度估计衡量的是果断性而非正确性，并且无法识别或折扣持续不可靠的智能体。我们提出贝叶斯辩证论证（BDA），它将委员会的类型化动作——谁提出、质疑或让步于哪个答案——视为具有每个智能体可靠性的经典标注者模型的观测。这一形式化将多智能体审议重构为可靠性估计问题，利用审议轨迹在持续对抗行为下推断智能体可靠性。通过根据推断出的智能体可靠性对证据进行加权，BDA在候选答案上产生经过校准的后验概率，同时允许持续不可靠的智能体被反转，而不仅仅是被投票压倒。在二分类和多分类基准上，BDA在零成本委员会聚合方法中取得了最佳校准，不需要额外的LLM调用，并且在持续对抗性联盟下提高了稳健性，同时在干净设置中保持竞争力。

Original abstract: A multi-LLM \emph{council} lets several large language models (LLMs) deliberate on a question and return an answer together with a confidence estimate. As these systems become increasingly used for reasoning, that confidence should represent a calibrated \emph{probability of being correct}, and the decision should remain robust when some agents are persistently unreliable. Existing \emph{council aggregation} methods fail on both fronts: their confidence estimates measure decisiveness rather than correctness, and they cannot identify or discount persistently unreliable agents. We introduce Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation (BDA), which treats the council's \emph{typed} moves---who proposed, challenged, or conceded which answer---as observations of a classical annotator model with \emph{per-agent} reliabilities. This formulation recasts multi-agent deliberation as a reliability estimation problem, using the deliberation trace to infer agent reliability under persistent adversarial behavior. By weighting evidence according to inferred agent reliability, BDA yields calibrated posterior probabilities over candidate answers while allowing persistently unreliable agents to be inverted rather than merely outvoted. Across binary and multi-class benchmarks, BDA achieves the best calibration among zero-cost council aggregation methods, requiring no additional LLM calls, and improves robustness under persistent adversarial coalitions while remaining competitive in clean settings.

</details>


### [84] [Global Coherence: When Every Agent Is Right and the Team Is Still Wrong - A Local-to-Global Semantic Foundation for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02036)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出多agent全局一致性问题与harness掌控共享状态/提交的运行语义，对多agent协作、状态一致性和可靠执行高度相关。

TL;DR: 论文提出全局连贯性问题与观察混叠不可能性定理，证明局部智能无法弥补共享全局状态缺失，并给出局部到全局运行时语义及九项实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体可各自做出局部有效决策，却联合产生无效结果；根因是共享状态缺失，而非模型智能不足，因此需要界定可保证全局有效动作的边界。

Method: 提出观察混叠不可能性定理以刻画边界；定义局部到全局运行时语义 X=(H,C,G,F;D)；并通过受控修订基准、TeamBench、tau2-bench Telecom 等九项研究检验失败及其边界。

Result: 可见决定事件时同模型得 40/40，隐藏时测试分支为 12--17/40，与 1/3 随机水平一致，恢复权威事实后回到 40/40；TeamBench 中普通团队 5/5 超预算、可见计数 4/5 违规、提交强制 0/5；tau2-bench Telecom 静默回滚后当前状态检查 0.07，而 harness 为 1.00；传统求解器拥有完整相关状态时与 harness 打平。

Conclusion: 反直觉结论是：局部智能不能替代缺失的全局状态；需要由 harness 拥有共享状态并管理提交，才能实现全局连贯。

中文摘要：AI 智能体可以各自做出局部有效的决策，但联合起来却产生无效结果。我们将此称为全局连贯性问题：这是共享状态的失败，而不仅仅是模型智能的失败。
我们的观察混叠不可能性定理给出了确切边界。一个策略能够保证有效动作，当且仅当所有产生相同观察的世界共享一个可容许动作。如果 k 个不可区分世界要求两两不相交的动作，那么最佳随机化最坏情况成功率为 1/k；更多的推理、角色、消息或样本都无法恢复缺失的区分。更强的模型可以在其上下文中更好地推理，但它无法看到上下文之外。
然后我们给出局部到全局的运行时语义 X = (H, C, G, F; D)：拓扑 H 记录重叠的作用域；范畴 C 支配改变状态的动作；群胚 G 保留可逆转换；层 F 检验局部视图能否粘合成一个世界；最小历史 D 只保留改变合法未来的区分。模型提出；harness 拥有共享状态并管理提交。
九项研究同时检验该失败及其边界。在一个受控修订基准上，同一个前沿模型在决定事件可见时得分为 40/40；当决定事件隐藏时，测试分支得分为 12--17/40，与随机概率 (1/3) 一致；恢复一个权威事实后回到 40/40。在 TeamBench 上，普通团队在 5/5 次运行中超出共享预算，可见实时计数仍留下 4/5 次违规，而提交强制执行留下 0/5。在 tau2-bench Telecom 中，静默回滚后当前状态检查得分为 0.07，而 harness 得分为 1.00。在传统求解器已经拥有完整相关状态的情况下，它如预测般与 harness 打平。反直觉的结论是：局部智能无法替代缺失的全局状态。

Original abstract: AI agents can each make locally valid decisions yet jointly produce an invalid result. We call this the global coherence problem: a failure of shared state, not merely of model intelligence.
  Our Observation-Aliasing Impossibility Theorem gives the exact boundary. A policy can guarantee a valid action exactly when all worlds producing the same observation share an admissible action. If k indistinguishable worlds require pairwise-disjoint actions, the best randomized worst-case success is 1/k; more reasoning, roles, messages, or samples cannot recover the missing distinction. A stronger model can reason better within its context, but it cannot see beyond it.
  We then give local-to-global runtime semantics X = (H, C, G, F; D): topology H records overlapping scopes; category C governs state-changing actions; groupoid G retains reversible translations; sheaf F tests whether local views glue into one world; and minimal history D keeps only distinctions that alter legal futures. Models propose; the harness owns shared state and governs commit.
  Nine studies test both the failure and its boundary. On a controlled revision benchmark, the same frontier model scores 40/40 when the deciding event is visible; when it is hidden, tested arms score 12--17/40, consistent with chance (1/3); restoring one authoritative fact returns 40/40. On TeamBench, ordinary teams exceed a shared budget in 5/5 runs, a visible live count leaves 4/5 violations, and commit enforcement leaves 0/5. In tau2-bench Telecom, current-state checks score 0.07 after silent reverts, while the harness scores 1.00. Where a conventional solver already owns the complete relevant state, it ties the harness as predicted. The counterintuitive conclusion is that local intelligence cannot substitute for missing global state.

</details>


### [85] [Mimir: Physics-Grounded LLM Agents for Long-Horizon Irrigation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02038)
*Yimeng Liu,Mi Zhang,Younsuk Dong,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程物理控制中的LLM agent框架，包含前向模拟、验证修正和持久上下文自改进，可迁移到长程agent与可靠行动选择。

TL;DR: Mimir 是面向长期灌溉控制的基于物理的LLM智能体，通过快/慢两个修复时间尺度实现有界自我改进，取得最低总控制成本，并比历史调度回放少用约51%灌溉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数LLM智能体证据来自回合式、即时反馈且失败可重置的任务；长期物理控制中动作改变未来状态、错误跨决策累积，且智能体必须在不改写安全物理规则的前提下从经验中改进。灌溉是这一机制的代表性场景。

Method: 提出 Mimir，围绕两个修复时间尺度组织：快时间尺度通过结构化物理接口和确定性模拟器，将LLM输出转为提案，并进行数值检查、修订和执行前的有界确定性动作选择；慢时间尺度将反复出现的失败模式固化为持久上下文原则以调节未来提案。物理模型、评估器和执行约束保持不变，并在多地点、多作物、多年份的共同回顾性评估器下评测。

Result: Mimir 在所评估参考方法中取得已报告的最低总控制成本，并比历史调度回放少用约51%灌溉。消融研究显示，移除前向模拟、经核验的修订或持久上下文会导致控制成本更高；模型规模和模型家族研究表明，增大LLM规模没有单调增益。

Conclusion: 持久运行的物理智能体可将语义推理与有界、证据驱动的自我改进结合，同时将物理真实性和执行器权限保留给显式数值机制。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体日益将推理、工具使用和行动结合起来，但大多数证据来自具有相对即时反馈且失败可重置的回合式任务。长期运行的物理控制运行于一种不同的机制：动作会改变未来状态，错误会跨决策累积，并且智能体必须从经验中改进，而不被允许改写使执行安全的物理规则。我们通过灌溉研究这一机制，其中每日决策在整个生长季与土壤-水分动力学相互作用。我们提出 Mimir，一个基于物理的LLM智能体，围绕两个修复时间尺度组织。在快时间尺度上，结构化物理接口和确定性模拟器将LLM输出转化为一个提案，我们对其数值检查、修订，并在执行前进行有界确定性动作选择。在慢时间尺度上，反复出现的失败模式被固化为持久的上下文原则，用以调节未来提案，同时物理模型、评估器和执行约束保持不变。在跨多个地点、作物和年份的共同回顾性评估器下，Mimir 在所评估的参考方法中取得了已报告的最低总控制成本，并且比历史调度回放少用约51%的灌溉。消融研究表明，当移除前向模拟、经核验的修订或持久上下文时，控制成本更高；模型规模和模型家族研究表明，增大LLM规模并未带来单调增益。由此得出的结论表明，持久性物理智能体可以将语义推理与有界、证据驱动的自我改进结合起来，同时将物理真实性和执行器权限保留给显式数值机制。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly combine reasoning, tool use, and action, but most evidence comes from episodic tasks with relatively immediate feedback and reset failures. Long-running physical control operates in a different regime: actions alter future states, errors compound across decisions, and an agent must improve from experience without being allowed to rewrite the physical rules that make execution safe. We study this regime through irrigation, where daily decisions interact with soil-water dynamics over entire growing seasons. We present Mimir, a physics-grounded LLM agent organized around two repair timescales. At the fast timescale, a structured physical interface and deterministic simulator turn an LLM output into a proposal that we numerically check, revise, and subject to bounded deterministic action selection before execution. At the slow timescale, recurrent failure patterns are consolidated into persistent contextual principles that condition future proposals, while the physical model, evaluator, and execution constraints remain immutable. Under a common retrospective evaluator across multiple sites, crops, and years, Mimir attains the lowest reported aggregate control cost among the evaluated references and uses about 51% less irrigation than the historical schedule replay. The ablation study show higher control cost when forward simulation, verified revision, or persistent context is removed; model-scale and model-family studies show no monotonic gain from increasing LLM size. The resulting lesson show that persistent physical agents can combine semantic reasoning with bounded, evidence-driven self-improvement while reserving physical truth and actuator authority for explicit numerical mechanisms.

</details>


### [86] [External Observers May See More Clearly: Cross-Model Span-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models via Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02066)
*Kingshuk Gupta,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 通过隐藏状态探测做细粒度幻觉检测，可用于agent输出验证与可靠性控制，但本身不是agent方法。

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于内部隐藏状态的跨度级幻觉检测框架，并发现外部观察者模型可匹配或超越生成器对自身幻觉起始的自检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为推理引擎时幻觉仍是关键脆弱性；现有内部状态探针多将幻觉检测简化为逐token二分类，无法捕捉语义漂移的结构化序列边界。

Method: 检查LLM生成过程中的逐层激活模式，以检测确切的幻觉起始和延续token，实现细粒度跨度级检测；进一步提出跨模型检测框架，让一个模型观察另一个模型生成所诱发的内部表示。

Result: 实验成功隔离幻觉起始，在极端类别不平衡下，PR-AUC相较随机基线显著提升；外部观察者能匹配或超过生成器的自检，即使观察者模型更小。

Conclusion: 细粒度内部状态检测可定位幻觉起始；自我检测并非幻觉起始定位的上限，跨模型观察具有提升检测性能的潜力。

中文摘要：随着大型语言模型（LLMs）日益充当基础推理引擎，其产生幻觉的倾向仍然是一个关键脆弱性。虽然近期的内部状态探测为缓慢的外部检索系统提供了一种有前景的替代方案，但它们在很大程度上将幻觉检测简化为逐token的二分类任务，未能捕捉语义漂移的结构化、序列化边界。在此，我们提出一个用于细粒度、跨度级幻觉检测的内部隐藏状态框架。通过检查逐层激活模式，我们尝试检测LLM生成中确切的幻觉起始和延续token。我们的实验表明，该方法成功隔离出幻觉起始，尽管存在极端类别不平衡，在精确率-召回率AUC上相较随机基线取得了显著提升。最终，我们提出一种新颖的跨模型检测框架，其中一个模型观察另一个模型生成所诱发的内部表示。我们发现，外部观察者能够匹配或超过生成器对其自身幻觉起始的自我检测，包括当观察者是较小模型时，这表明自我检测并非起始定位的上限。

Original abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as foundational reasoning engines, their tendency to hallucinate remains a critical vulnerability. While recent internal state probes offer a promising alternative to slow external retrieval systems, they largely reduce hallucination detection to a token-wise binary classification task, failing to capture the structured, sequential boundaries of semantic drift. Here, we introduce an internal hidden state framework for fine-grained, span-level hallucination detection. By inspecting layer-wise activation patterns, we attempt to detect the exact hallucination onset and continuation tokens in an LLM generation. Our experiments show that this approach successfully isolates hallucination onsets, achieving substantial improvements in Precision-Recall AUC over random baselines despite extreme class imbalance. Ultimately, we propose a novel cross-model detection framework in which one model observes the internal representations elicited by another model's generation. We find that an external observer can match or exceed a generator's self-detection of its own hallucination onsets, including when the observer is the smaller model, suggesting that self-detection is not the ceiling for onset localisation.

</details>


### [87] [Causal Memory Policy: Making Memory Utility Identifiable by Intervening on Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02070)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出因果记忆策略，通过干预检索识别记忆效用，对agent记忆保留、经验复用和决策规则有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文指出记忆增强LLM中仅基于已检索记忆估计效用会导致检索层面的正值性违背，使未检索记忆的效用不可识别；提出CMP通过干预检索并采用自归一化逆倾向性加权恢复识别，给出因果分解、无偏性与方差、不可逆操作最优决策规则，并在实验中验证识别失败及CMP的改进，同时揭示已识别效用仍不足以外推到未见查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆保留系统通过估计记忆对任务性能的影响来决定保留哪些记忆，但估计完全依赖已检索记忆；从未被检索的记忆在存储层干预下结果相同，效用不可识别，而仅检查记忆操作的诊断无法发现该问题。

Method: 提出因果记忆策略（CMP）：在检索层面进行干预，为以已知倾向性采样的记忆保留固定数量的上下文槽位，并在平衡分配设计下用自归一化逆倾向性加权估计记忆效用；理论上证明记忆效用经由检索的因果分解、估计量的无偏性与精确方差，以及不可逆操作下的最优决策规则。

Result: 在LongMemEval上54%、LoCoMo上67%的必需记忆识别失败，且该失败在已部署记忆系统中仍存在；CMP将必需与非必需记忆的区分度从0.54 AUC提升至0.66 AUC；逐查询效用在被估计查询上达0.78 AUC，但保留策略可用的聚合无法预测记忆在未见查询上的价值。

Conclusion: 检索层面的正值性违背会破坏记忆效用识别，CMP可恢复识别并改善区分度；但仅靠已识别记忆效用仍不足以支持保留决策，因为逐查询效用难以聚合泛化到未见查询，说明记忆保留还需解决跨查询价值预测问题。

中文摘要：记忆增强的大语言模型必须决定保留哪些记忆，而近期系统通过估计每个记忆对任务性能的影响来做到这一点。然而，这些估计完全依赖于被检索到的记忆。当一个记忆从未被检索时，存储层干预会产生相同结果，使其效用无法被识别。这是检索层面的正值性违背，对于仅检查记忆操作的诊断不可见。我们提出因果记忆策略（Causal Memory Policy, CMP），一个因果框架，通过干预检索本身来恢复可识别性，为以已知倾向性采样的记忆保留固定数量的上下文槽位。CMP在平衡分配设计下通过自归一化逆倾向性加权估计记忆效用。我们证明了记忆效用经由检索的因果分解、估计量的无偏性与精确方差，以及不可逆操作下的最优决策规则。经验上，在LongMemEval上54%的必需记忆识别失败，在LoCoMo上67%，并且这种失败在已部署的记忆系统中持续存在。CMP将必需记忆与非必需记忆之间的区分度从0.54 AUC提高到0.66 AUC。最后，我们表明，仅识别出的记忆效用不足以用于保留决策：逐查询效用在被估计的那个查询上达到0.78 AUC，但保留策略可用的任何聚合方式都无法预测一个记忆在未见查询上的价值。代码可在以下网址获取：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cmp-release-D0C3/。

Original abstract: Memory-augmented large language models must decide which memories to retain, and recent systems do so by estimating each memory's effect on task performance. However, these estimates rely entirely on retrieved memories. When a memory is never retrieved, store-level interventions produce identical outcomes, leaving its utility unidentified. This is a retrieval-level positivity violation, invisible to diagnostics that examine only memory operations. We introduce Causal Memory Policy (CMP), a causal framework that restores identification by intervening on retrieval itself, reserving a fixed number of context slots for memories sampled with known propensities. CMP estimates memory utility by self-normalized inverse propensity weighting under a balanced assignment design. We prove the causal factorization of memory utility through retrieval, the unbiasedness and exact variance of the estimator, and the optimal decision rule under irreversible operations. Empirically, identification fails for 54% of required memories on LongMemEval and 67% on LoCoMo, and the failure persists in a deployed memory system. CMP improves discrimination between required and non-required memories from 0.54 to 0.66 AUC. Finally, we show that identified memory utility alone is insufficient for retention decisions: per-query utility reaches 0.78 AUC on the query for which it is estimated, yet no aggregation available to a retention policy predicts a memory's value on unseen queries. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cmp-release-D0C3/.

</details>


### [88] [VISTA: A Visual Harness for Reasoning in an Interactive World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02200)
*Qiushi Han,Keya Hu,Linlu Qiu,Cathy Wu,Kaiming He*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Tech report. An early version of this manuscript was in a blogpost published in Aug 5, 2026: https://vista-research.github.io/

Selection reason: 提出通用多模态agent的视觉harness与无损记忆/主动检索机制，对coding agent的harness设计与长程记忆有可迁移启发。

TL;DR: VISTA 是一种视觉 harness，赋予通用多模态模型长时程视觉与无损视觉记忆，在 ARC-AGI-3 等视觉任务上显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型具备强推理能力，但需要合适的 harness 才能在不同交互环境中释放潜力，并在长时程视觉任务中保留过去观察。

Method: 提出 VISTA：模型通过视觉观察直接感知环境，维护原始形式的无损视觉记忆，并可在推理时主动检索和重组视觉输入；设计简洁，可低适配扩展到多样视觉环境。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 上，Claude Opus 5.0 的相对人类动作效率从 40.68 提升到 100.00，完成全部 25 个公开游戏，动作数比首次人类参与者少 57.4%；在另外三个视觉游戏/谜题基准上大幅优于极简 harness 基线。

Conclusion: VISTA 有望成为通用视觉 harness，推动多模态智能体在复杂视觉环境中的发展。

中文摘要：我们表明，多模态模型具备强大的推理能力，而一个适当的 harness 能够释放其潜力，以解决多样交互环境中的任务。我们提出 VISTA，一种视觉 harness，使通用多模态模型具备长时程视觉能力。VISTA 允许模型通过视觉观察直接感知环境，并维护无损视觉记忆，以原始形式保存过去的观察。模型可以在推理时主动检索这些观察并重新组织其视觉输入。在 ARC-AGI-3 上，VISTA 将 Claude Opus 5.0 的相对人类动作效率得分从 40.68 提升至完美的 100.00，模型完成了全部 25 个公开游戏，且相比首次参与的人类参与者少用 57.4% 的动作。VISTA 的简洁设计还使其能够以极小的适配自然扩展到多样的视觉环境。在另外三个涵盖多样视觉游戏和谜题的基准上，它大幅优于使用相同底层模型但仅配以极简 harness 的基线。我们的结果凸显了 VISTA 作为通用视觉 harness 的潜力，可推动多模态智能体在复杂视觉环境中取得进展。

Original abstract: We show that multimodal models possess strong reasoning abilities and that an appropriate harness can unlock their potential to solve tasks across diverse interactive environments. We introduce VISTA, a visual harness that gives a general-purpose multimodal model long-horizon vision. VISTA allows the model to directly perceive the environment through visual observations and maintains a lossless visual memory that preserves past observations in their original form. The model can actively retrieve these observations and reorganize its visual input as it reasons. On ARC-AGI-3, VISTA improves Claude Opus 5.0's Relative Human Action Efficiency score from 40.68 to a perfect 100.00, with the model completing all 25 public games using 57.4% fewer actions than first-time human participants. VISTA's simple design also allows it to extend naturally to diverse visual environments with minimal adaptation. Across three additional benchmarks covering a diverse range of visual games and puzzles, it substantially outperforms baselines using the same underlying model with minimal harnesses. Our results highlight VISTA's potential as a general-purpose visual harness for advancing multimodal agents in complex visual environments.

</details>


### [89] [ScholarCatalyst: A Benchmark for Retrieving Papers That Inspire New Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02202)
*Sohyeon Kim,Yoonho Lee,Bo Liu,Dayoon Ko,Rulin Shao,Seungone Kim,Graham Neubig,Pang Wei Koh,Aakanksha Chowdhery,Akari Asai,Omar Khattab,Yejin Choi,Gunhee Kim,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 57 pages

Selection reason: 构建检索型agentic搜索基准并发现代理搜索未优于嵌入检索，对检索增强agent的能力边界与评测设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 论文构建 ScholarCatalyst，由184位主要作者标注207篇近期计算机科学论文的相关先前工作，并设置检索任务；结果显示智能体搜索并不优于嵌入检索，需新训练方法赋予模型专家式文献检索直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究科学家如何识别新问题所需的先前想法；即使AI在开放问题上取得进展，这方面仍落后。作者构建可扩展的作者标注数据来刻画该技能。

Method: 通过自动化流程让184位主要作者对207篇近期计算机科学论文进行标注，标记哪些候选论文已经或本可以推进其项目并给出理由；提出基于作者判断的检索任务：给定初始研究问题，仅从项目开始时的文献中检索论文；比较嵌入检索、智能体搜索及基于 Claude Fable 5.1 的智能体。

Result: 智能体搜索不优于嵌入检索（0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20）；基于 Claude Fable 5.1 的智能体也仅达0.51 R@20。

Conclusion: 结果表明需要新的训练方法，使模型具备在广泛语料中搜索的专家直觉；ScholarCatalyst 是迈向能根据半成形想法找到所需先前研究的科学智能体的一步。

中文摘要：什么使伟大的科学家伟大？即使AI系统开始在开放问题上取得进展，科学家在感知一个新问题需要哪些先前想法方面仍远远领先于它们，这些想法埋藏在不断增长的研究档案中。为了研究这种技能，我们借助那些亲身知道哪些早期工作推进了他们已完成项目的研究者，论文则充当其中想法的指针。利用我们使作者标注可扩展的自动化流程，我们构建了 ScholarCatalyst，让207篇近期计算机科学论文的184位主要作者标注哪些候选论文已经或本可以推进他们的项目，每篇都附有详细理由。我们引入一个带有作者提供判断的检索任务：给定一个初始研究问题，仅从项目开始时已有的文献中检索这些论文。尽管将同一检索器作为工具调用，智能体式搜索并不优于嵌入检索（0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20）。即使一个基于 Claude Fable 5.1 构建的智能体——它可能在训练中见过这些已完成的论文——也只达到0.51 R@20。这些结果凸显出需要新的训练方法，使模型具备在广泛语料库中搜索的专家直觉。我们将 ScholarCatalyst 设想为迈向科学智能体的一步，这些智能体能够接收一个半成形的想法并指向其所需要的先前研究。

Original abstract: What makes great scientists great? Even as AI systems start to make progress on open problems, scientists remain far ahead of them at sensing which prior idea, buried in an ever-growing archive of research, a new problem needs. To study this skill, we draw on researchers who know firsthand which earlier work advanced their completed projects, with papers serving as pointers to the ideas within. Using our automated pipeline that makes author annotation scalable, we build ScholarCatalyst by having 184 lead authors of 207 recent computer science papers label which candidates did or could have advanced their project, each with a detailed rationale. We introduce a retrieval task with author-provided judgments: given an initial research question, retrieve these papers from only the literature available when the project began. Agentic search does no better than embedding retrieval (0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20) despite calling that same retriever as a tool. Even an agent built on Claude Fable 5.1, which may have seen the completed papers during training, reaches only 0.51 R@20. These results highlight the need for new training recipes that equip models with expert intuition for searching broad corpora. We envision ScholarCatalyst as a step toward scientific agents that can take a half-formed idea and point to the prior research it needs.

</details>
